- **Research Test Writer**: Writes tests for research code including data validation, statistical computation verification, and output format checks ([Nathandela/dark-research-lab](https://github.com/Nathandela/dark-research-lab/tree/HEAD/go/internal/setup/templates/agent-role-skills/test-writer) · ★3)
- **Research Topic From Conversation**: Research a topic that surfaced in a Harmony conversation and produce a structured briefing with findings, sources, and next steps — grounded in what was actually discussed. Trigger on: research this, look into, brief me on, dig into, what do we know about, can you find out more about. ([heyharmony/skills](https://github.com/heyharmony/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/research) · ★1)
- **Research Trace Authoring**: Turn reusable research workflows into annotated traces. ([idanmann10/vivarium-world](https://github.com/idanmann10/vivarium-world/tree/HEAD/domains/research/skills/research-trace-authoring) · ★1)
- **research-01-model-architecture**: Model architecture deep-dive — transformers, attention, LLM internals, and implementations (litgpt, nanogpt, mamba, rwkv, torchtitan). ([amutaal122/ai-engineer-master](https://github.com/amutaal122/ai-engineer-master/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research-01-model-architecture))
- **research-02-tokenization**: Tokenization strategies — BPE, sentencepiece, wordpiece, vocabulary building, and tokenizer training. ([amutaal122/ai-engineer-master](https://github.com/amutaal122/ai-engineer-master/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research-02-tokenization))
- **research-03-fine-tuning**: Fine-tuning techniques — SFT, LoRA, PEFT, instruction tuning with axolotl, llama-factory, and unsloth. ([amutaal122/ai-engineer-master](https://github.com/amutaal122/ai-engineer-master/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research-03-fine-tuning))
- **research-04-mechanistic-interpretability**: Mechanistic interpretability — SAE, circuits, activation patching, probing with nnsight, pyvene, saelens, transformer-lens. ([amutaal122/ai-engineer-master](https://github.com/amutaal122/ai-engineer-master/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research-04-mechanistic-interpretability))
- **research-05-data-processing**: Training data processing — curation, cleaning, quality filters with nemo-curator and ray-data. ([amutaal122/ai-engineer-master](https://github.com/amutaal122/ai-engineer-master/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research-05-data-processing))
- **research-06-post-training**: Post-training alignment — RLHF, DPO, GRPO, reward models with openrlhf, TRL, verl, simpo. ([amutaal122/ai-engineer-master](https://github.com/amutaal122/ai-engineer-master/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research-06-post-training))
- **research-07-safety-alignment**: Safety and alignment — constitutional AI, guardrails, llamaguard, red teaming, prompt-guard. ([amutaal122/ai-engineer-master](https://github.com/amutaal122/ai-engineer-master/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research-07-safety-alignment))
- **research-08-distributed-training**: Distributed training — FSDP, DeepSpeed, Megatron, multi-GPU, model parallelism with accelerate and ray-train. ([amutaal122/ai-engineer-master](https://github.com/amutaal122/ai-engineer-master/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research-08-distributed-training))
- **research-09-infrastructure**: ML infrastructure — GPU clusters, cloud GPU rental with lambda-labs, modal, and skypilot. ([amutaal122/ai-engineer-master](https://github.com/amutaal122/ai-engineer-master/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research-09-infrastructure))
- **research-10-optimization**: Model optimization — quantization (AWQ, GGUF, GPTQ, BitsAndBytes, HQQ), pruning, compression. ([amutaal122/ai-engineer-master](https://github.com/amutaal122/ai-engineer-master/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research-10-optimization))
- **research-11-evaluation**: LLM evaluation — benchmarks, eval harness, bigcode-eval, nemo-evaluator, standardized assessment. ([amutaal122/ai-engineer-master](https://github.com/amutaal122/ai-engineer-master/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research-11-evaluation))
- **research-12-inference-serving**: Inference serving — vLLM, sglang, tensorrt-llm, llama.cpp for high-throughput production deployment. ([amutaal122/ai-engineer-master](https://github.com/amutaal122/ai-engineer-master/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research-12-inference-serving))
- **research-13-mlops**: MLOps — experiment tracking with mlflow, tensorboard, weights-and-biases, model registry. ([amutaal122/ai-engineer-master](https://github.com/amutaal122/ai-engineer-master/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research-13-mlops))
- **research-14-agents**: Agent frameworks — crewai, langchain, llamaindex, autogpt for multi-agent orchestration. ([amutaal122/ai-engineer-master](https://github.com/amutaal122/ai-engineer-master/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research-14-agents))
- **research-15-rag**: RAG pipelines — retrieval, vector databases (chroma, faiss, pinecone, qdrant), sentence-transformers. ([amutaal122/ai-engineer-master](https://github.com/amutaal122/ai-engineer-master/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research-15-rag))
- **research-16-prompt-engineering**: Advanced prompt engineering — dspy, guidance, instructor, outlines for structured LLM output. ([amutaal122/ai-engineer-master](https://github.com/amutaal122/ai-engineer-master/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research-16-prompt-engineering))
- **research-17-observability**: LLM observability — tracing, monitoring with langsmith and phoenix. ([amutaal122/ai-engineer-master](https://github.com/amutaal122/ai-engineer-master/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research-17-observability))
- **research-18-multimodal**: Multimodal models — vision-language, audio, image generation (audiocraft, blip-2, clip, llava, SAM, stable-diffusion, whisper). ([amutaal122/ai-engineer-master](https://github.com/amutaal122/ai-engineer-master/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research-18-multimodal))
- **research-19-emerging-techniques**: Emerging techniques — speculative decoding, knowledge distillation, model merging, MoE, long context. ([amutaal122/ai-engineer-master](https://github.com/amutaal122/ai-engineer-master/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research-19-emerging-techniques))
- **research-20-ml-paper-writing**: ML paper writing — structure, related work, ablation, academic conventions for arxiv/NeurIPS/ICML. ([amutaal122/ai-engineer-master](https://github.com/amutaal122/ai-engineer-master/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research-20-ml-paper-writing))
